## drawdown_maximo (sim)

- Maximum Drawdown : This is the maximum allowable loss from the peak of your account balance.  
  <sub> · [8528339-understanding-evaluation-parameters-at-myfundedfutur](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/8528339-understanding-evaluation-parameters-at-myfundedfutures)</sub>
- Intraday Drawdown (Rapid Plans) : A trailing drawdown is a dynamic loss limit that increases as your account's equity reaches new highs, but does not decrease when losses occur.  
  <sub> · [8528339-understanding-evaluation-parameters-at-myfundedfutur](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/8528339-understanding-evaluation-parameters-at-myfundedfutures)</sub>
- MyFundedFutures uses intraday drawdown to monitor a trader's risk exposure throughout the trading day.  
  <sub> · [8528339-understanding-evaluation-parameters-at-myfundedfutur](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/8528339-understanding-evaluation-parameters-at-myfundedfutures)</sub>
- Minimum Trading Days : Rapid and Pro evaluations require traders to trade for a minimum number of 2 days to ensure they are genuinely engaged and not just taking wild risks hoping for a lucky break.​ Start your Evaluation today Importance and Impact on Trading Understanding these parameters is not just about compliance; it's about internalizing the principles of disciplined trading: Risk Management : The Maximum Drawdown, Intraday and EOD Drawdown parameters are quintessential for instilling robust risk management habits.  
  <sub>2 days · [8528339-understanding-evaluation-parameters-at-myfundedfutur](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/8528339-understanding-evaluation-parameters-at-myfundedfutures)</sub>

  <sub> · [12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained)</sub>
- How It Works At the funded stage, the trailing drawdown is calculated intraday based on your peak balance, which includes both realized and unrealized gains.  
  <sub> · [12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained)</sub>
- At the start of your evaluation or funded account, My Funded Futures sets the following: Starting Balance: For example, $50,000 Maximum Trailing Drawdown Amount: For example, $2,000 Your initial equity limit is calculated as follows: Starting Balance − Trailing Drawdown → $50,000 − $2,000 = $48,000 When your account reaches a new equity high, including unrealized profits, the trailing threshold adjusts upward accordingly: New Equity High: $51,000 New Drawdown Limit: $51,000 − $2,000 = $49,000 If your account value declines, even during an open trade, the drawdown limit remains unchanged.  
  <sub>$50,000, $2,000, $50,000, $2,000, $48,000, $51,000 · [12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained)</sub>
- However, if your equity, including open trades, falls below the most recently adjusted drawdown limit, your account will breach the maximum loss limit.  
  <sub> · [12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained)</sub>
- Traders can monitor the remaining intraday maximum drawdown limit while executing trades on their trading platforms.  
  <sub> · [12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained)</sub>
- For Tradovate accounts, keep track of your trailing drawdown doing the following: Sign in to your Tradovate account.  
  <sub> · [12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained)</sub>
- This screen will need to be manually formatted to show the needed columns displaying your trailing drawdown.  
  <sub> · [12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/12802721-intraday-drawdown-explained)</sub>
- From drop down options, select 'DRAWDOWN AUTO LIQ LEVEL' to monitor current trailing drawdown limit and 'DIST DRAWDOWN' to monitor how much drawdown remains before reaching max drawdown.

- (Pro Plan One day pass has no consistency rule) ​Our Builder plans have no consistency rules in the evaluation stage and can be passed in 1 day. *** Builder plans have a 50% consistency rule in the simulated funded stage. *** This consistency rule means no single day's profit should exceed 30% or 50% of your total evaluation profits made.

- This rule promotes discipline and consistent trading habits which highlights a prop firm's commitment to encourage responsible trading. ​ ​ Example on how the 50% consistency rule works: Total Profit Target on a 50k account: $3,000 Max Profit a Trader should achieve: $1,500 Calculation is: Total profit target / 2 = Max Daily Profit ​ Important note: Exceeding the consistency targets does not breach the account.

## ganancia_extraordinaria (filtro)

- Minimum Trading Days : Rapid and Pro evaluations require traders to trade for a minimum number of 2 days to ensure they are genuinely engaged and not just taking wild risks hoping for a lucky break.​ Start your Evaluation today Importance and Impact on Trading Understanding these parameters is not just about compliance; it's about internalizing the principles of disciplined trading: Risk Management : The Maximum Drawdown, Intraday and EOD Drawdown parameters are quintessential for instilling robust risk management habits.

- Trades may be placed beginning at 6:00 PM EST when the Globex session opens and can remain open until the New York session closes at 4:10 PM EST.  
  <sub>6:00, 4:10 · [9558251-permitted-times-to-trade](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/9558251-permitted-times-to-trade)</sub>
- At MyFundedFutures, any open positions will be automatically closed at 4:10 PM EST on regular (non-holiday) trading days.  
  <sub>4:10 · [9558251-permitted-times-to-trade](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/9558251-permitted-times-to-trade)</sub>
- Holiday trading hours will be communicated to traders including closure of open positions. ​ As a result, you do not need to be concerned about unintentionally violating this rule due to an open position.  
  <sub> · [9558251-permitted-times-to-trade](https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/9558251-permitted-times-to-trade)</sub>
- Note: Make sure that you don’t force a trade multiple times on the market after 4:10 PM EST, as this will sometimes cause the order to go through and disqualify your trading account.
